# Agent profiles with model fallbacks

**Status:** accepted. It replaces the per‑project, per‑phase `{ kind, model, effort, args }` profile of [ADR 0008](0008-config-proyecto.md) and [effort per phase](effort-por-fase.md), and the registry‑order fallback of [ADR 0016](0016-proveedores-agente.md) and [settings‑proveedores](settings-proveedores.md).

## Context

A project configured one provider, model, effort and args per phase, and the only fallback was "try the other registered providers in registry order" when a CLI did not start. When a model hit its rate limit or usage limit, or the agent exited, the task retried the same model or escalated. // Decorative SVG icons for the actions on a task; the button provides the accessible name.
import type { ComponentProps } from "react";

function Icon(props: ComponentProps<"svg"> & { name: string }) {
  const { name, children, ...rest } = props;
  return (
    <svg
      aria-hidden="true"
      data-icon={name}
      width={14}
      height={14}
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="currentColor"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth={2}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      {...rest}
    >
      {children}
    </svg>
  );
}

### Verdict

- Verdict: **reject**
- Risk: medium

#### Criteria

- AC-1 · pass · client.test.ts 'the appearance migration gives every existing profile an icon and a color of the sets' green in pnpm test
- AC-2 · pass · domain randomAppearance tests, db assigns random when missing, server POST without icon/color test green
- AC-3 · pass · schemas project.test enum rejection, db and server 400 invalid_input with path tests green
- AC-4 · pass · db and server 'only a color' tests green, event agent_profile.updated asserted
- AC-5 · pass · db and server 'without icon or color keeps stored' tests green
- AC-6 · pass · profiles.test 'the new profile dialog preselects an icon and a color...' green
- AC-7 · pass · profiles.test 'editing a profile picks another icon...' green
- AC-8 · pass · agent-badges.test 'each row shows the badge of its own Run's profile' green
- AC-9 · fail · agent-badge.tsx useAgentProfiles ignores loading and error: while agent-profiles loads, a Run or task with a valid profile shows the neutral 'no profile' badge (mustFix 1)
- AC-10 · pass · agent-badges.test 'the Agent column shows the badge of the latest Run's profile' green
- AC-11 · fail · queue.tsx Agent column shows the 'no profile' mark while agent-profiles loads, even when the project assigns a profile (mustFix 1)
- AC-12 · pass · agent-badges.test and attribution.test green; attribution only for actor agent
- AC-13 · pass · agent-badges.test and attribution.test (specVersionRun) green
- AC-14 · fail · inbox.tsx:293 passes kind null while the runs query loads, so a blocked agent shows 'no profile' (mustFix 2)
- AC-15 · pass · agent-badge.test, theme-tokens.test (AA contrast both themes), profiles.test labels from dictionary green; minor test findings below

#### Reviewers

- `lunasq-security-reviewer` · NO FINDINGS
- `lunasq-ux-reviewer` · 6 findings
  - [major] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:25 · AC-9 · useAgentProfiles never checks isLoading or isError: while the agent-profiles query loads or fails, a profiled Run or queued task shows the neutral 'no profile' badge
  - [major] packages/web/src/pages/inbox.tsx:293 · AC-14 · AgentBadge gets kind null while the runs query loads, so a blocked agent shows 'Sin perfil / No profile' instead of its badge or the neutral kind
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/profile-form-dialog.tsx:331 · AC-15 · Each selector is a fieldset with a legend and a radiogroup with the same aria-label, so screen readers announce the group name twice
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/profiles.tsx:878 · AC-15 · ProfileChip (role img, aria-label 'Agente <name>') sits inside the card h2, so each heading is announced as an image instead of the profile name
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:137 · AC-15 · RunAgent neutral badge: the visible model text is aria-hidden and the accessible name omits it, so screen readers miss the model shown to sighted users
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/queue.tsx:931 · AC-11 · If the projects query fails or the task's project is missing, the Agent cell shows a bare '...' that never resolves and has no explanation
- `lunasq-ui-reviewer` · 2 findings
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:14 · AC-15 · CHIP copies the base classes of badgeVariants in components/ui/badge.tsx and NEUTRAL copies its secondary variant; they will drift apart
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/profile-form-dialog.tsx:179 · AC-15 · Icon and color radio labels use has-[:focus-visible]:outline-2 without outline-ring; other focus styles use the ring/primary token
- `lunasq-code-reviewer` · 2 findings
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.test.tsx:35 · AC-15 · Test 'every icon and color of the sets has a glyph and a class' only asserts PROFILE_ICONS.length > 0; it never checks each icon has a glyph
  - [minor] packages/web/src/theme-tokens.test.ts:146 · AC-15 · Test name and messages are in Spanish; AGENTS.md requires tests in English

#### Must-fix

- [major] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:25 · AC-9 · useAgentProfiles never checks isLoading or isError: while the agent-profiles query loads or fails, a profiled Run or queued task shows the neutral 'no profile' badge. Fix: show a loading placeholder (like the '...' used elsewhere) and a neutral fallback only once the list has loaded or failed, and cover it with a test that delays the profiles query. · source: lunasq-ux-reviewer
- [major] packages/web/src/pages/inbox.tsx:293 · AC-14 · AgentBadge gets kind null while the runs query loads, so a blocked agent shows 'Sin perfil / No profile' instead of its badge or the neutral kind. Fix: do not render the no-profile mark until the Run is known, and add a test for the Inbox while runs load. · source: lunasq-ux-reviewer
- [major] packages/web/src/pages/queue.tsx:931 · AC-11 · Queue Agent column: with the agent-profiles query loading, a task whose project assigns a profile shows the no-profile mark. The same root cause as the agent-badge.tsx:25 must-fix; the fix must also cover the Queue path, with a test for a delayed profiles query. · source: lunasq-ux-reviewer

Approved criteria (AC-n):
- AC-1 migration gives every existing profile an icon from PROFILE_ICONS and a color from PROFILE_COLORS; name and models unchanged.
- AC-2 create without icon/color stores random ones from the sets; agent_profile.created published.
- AC-3 icon/color outside the sets rejected with 400 invalid_input and path icon/color; nothing stored.
- AC-4 update with only color changes color, keeps icon/name/models; agent_profile.updated published.
- AC-5 update without icon/color keeps stored values.
- AC-6 new profile dialog preselects icon and color, preview shows badge, selector changes update preview and are sent.
- AC-7 edit dialog: picking another icon sends it in PATCH; Profiles tab shows new badge.
- AC-8 Runs table: each row shows badge of its own Run's profile.
- AC-9 Run with null or deleted profile: neutral badge with agent kind; nothing fails.
- AC-10 In progress Agent column shows badge of latest Run's profile.
- AC-11 Queue: badge of profile the project assigns to next phase, or no-profile mark.
- AC-12 Event log: badge on agent-actor events only, from the Run open at that time.
- AC-13 Spec panel: badge on agent-authored versions, none on human ones.
- AC-14 Inbox: badge of blocked agent's Run profile.
- AC-15 Labels from active dictionary (es and en); accessible name with profile name; color via theme token defined for light and dark.
Invariants: exactly one icon and one color from the closed sets; changing appearance does not interrupt Runs or change dispatch.

Test commands in the implementation handoff: pnpm typecheck, pnpm lint, pnpm test.

Focus on the production code and tests: correctness, design, compliance with the criteria above, and whether the tests really exercise each criterion (flag trivial or tautological assertions, skipped tests, weakened existing tests).   if (isUniqueName(error)) {
    throw new AgentProfileRepositoryError(
      "conflict",
      `an agent profile named ${JSON.stringify(name)} already exists`,
      { cause: error },
    );
  }
  throw error;
}

export function readAgentProfile(db: Reader, profileId: string): AgentProfile | null {

   */
  update(profileId: string, patch: AgentProfileUpdate): AgentProfile | null {
    const values = AgentProfileUpdate.parse(patch);
    try {
      return this.db.transaction((tx) => {
        const current = readAgentProfile(tx, profileId);
        if (!current) return null;
        const changed =
          (values.name !== undefined && values.name !== current.name) ||
          (values.models !== undefined &&
            JSON.stringify(values.models) !== JSON.stringify(current.models)) ||
          (values.icon !== undefined && values.icon !== current.icon) ||
          (values.color !== undefined && values.color !== current.color);
        if (!changed) return current;
        const [row] = tx
          .update(agentProfiles)
          .set(values)
          .where(eq(agentProfiles.id, profileId))
          .returning()
          .all();
        if (!row) throw new Error("agent profile update without a row");
        const profile = toProfile(row);
        this.publish(tx, AGENT_PROFILE_UPDATED, profile);
        return profile;
      });
    } catch (error) {
      translate(error, values.name);
    }
  }

function priority(entry: Entry): string {
  const base = entry.task.priority;
  return entry.effectivePriority !== null && entry.effectivePriority !== base
    ? `${base} → ${entry.effectivePriority}`
    : String(base);
}

/** Icons only, with the task in the aria-label: pause if it can be paused, resume if it is paused or escalated. */
function ActionsCell({ row }: CellContext<Entry, unknown>) {
  const { task } = row.original;
  if (canPause(task.state)) return <PauseTaskButton task={task} variant="icon" />;
  if (canResume(task.state)) return <ResumeTaskButton task={task} variant="icon" />;
  return null;
}

#### Criteria

- AC-4 · pass · db and server 'only a color' tests green, agent_profile.updated asserted
- AC-5 · pass · db and server 'keeps the stored ones' tests green
- AC-6 · pass · profiles.test new profile dialog test green
- AC-7 · pass · profiles.test edit icon test green
- AC-8 · pass · agent-badges.test Runs row test green
- AC-9 · fail · agent-badge.tsx useAgentProfiles reports loading only from isPending and find returns null on error, so a Run with a valid profile shows the neutral 'no profile' label when agent-profiles fails (same failure case as the round-1 must-fix)
- AC-10 · pass · agent-badges.test In progress Agent column test green
- AC-11 · fail · queue.tsx:101 passes project.agentProfiles[phase] to AgentBadge; when agent-profiles fails, find returns null and the cell shows the no-profile mark for an assigned profile
- AC-12 · pass · agent-badges.test and attribution.test green
- AC-13 · pass · agent-badges.test and attribution.test green
- AC-14 · fail · inbox.tsx:306 sets pending from runs.isPending only; if the runs query fails, run is undefined, kind is null and a blocked agent shows the no-profile mark
- AC-15 · pass · agent-badge.test, theme-tokens.test (AA both themes) and profiles.test labels from dictionary green; accessibility and focus findings are minor

#### Reviewers

- `lunasq-security-reviewer` · skipped · The diff adds HTTP fields icon and color, both closed zod enums rejected with 400 invalid_input (AC-3 tests); no new auth, process, path or dependency surface
- `lunasq-ux-reviewer` · 6 findings
  - [major] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:27 · AC-11 · useAgentProfiles only reports isPending; on query failure find returns null and Queue shows the no-profile mark for an assigned profile
  - [major] packages/web/src/pages/inbox.tsx:306 · AC-14 · pending covers only runs.isPending; on runs failure the blocked agent shows the no-profile mark
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/queue.tsx:904 · AC-11 · If the projects query fails or the task's project is missing, the Agent cell shows a bare '…' with no explanation
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:118 · AC-15 · Neutral Run badge: visible 'claude · opus' text is aria-hidden and the accessible name drops the model
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/profiles.tsx:861 · AC-15 · ProfileChip role=img is the only content of the card h2, so the heading is announced as an image named 'Agente X'
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/profile-form-dialog.tsx:314 · AC-15 · Each selector fieldset legend duplicates the inner radiogroup aria-label, so the group name is announced twice (also at line 345)
- `lunasq-ui-reviewer` · 3 findings
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/profile-form-dialog.tsx:179 · AC-15 · Icon and color labels use has-[:focus-visible]:outline-2 with no outline color, so focus is drawn in the text color instead of the primary focus ring used elsewhere (also line 210)
  - [minor] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:14 · AC-15 · CHIP copies badgeVariants base classes and NEUTRAL copies its secondary variant; they will drift apart
  - [minor] packages/web/src/pages/queue.tsx:772 · AC-11 · While projects load the Agent column shows a bare '…' instead of the AgentBadge loading placeholder used elsewhere
- `lunasq-code-reviewer` · 3 findings

  - [minor] packages/web/src/theme-tokens.test.ts:776 · AC-15 · New test title and assertion messages are in Spanish; AGENTS.md requires English tests
  - [minor] packages/db/src/client.test.ts:249 · AC-1 · The migration test says names are unchanged but only checks 40 distinct names, never compares them with the inserted profile-N values

#### Must-fix

- [major] packages/web/src/components/agent-badge.tsx:27 · AC-11 · useAgentProfiles reports only isPending; if the agent-profiles query fails, find returns null and queue.tsx:101 shows the no-profile mark for a project that assigns a profile. Fix: expose isError too and do not render the no-profile mark on error (show the placeholder or a neutral badge that does not claim 'no profile'), and cover it with a test that fails the profiles query. · source: lunasq-ux-reviewer
- [major] packages/web/src/pages/inbox.tsx:306 · AC-14 · pending covers only runs.isPending; if the runs query fails, the blocked agent shows the no-profile mark instead of its badge or the neutral kind. Fix: treat a failed runs query like the other Inbox queries (no 'no profile' claim) and add a test with a failing runs query. · source: lunasq-ux-reviewer

/** The chip of an appearance; also the live preview of the profile form. */
export function ProfileChip({
  name,
  icon,
  color,
  className,
}: {
  name: string;
  icon: ProfileIcon;
  color: ProfileColor;
  className?: string | undefined;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  return (
    <span
      role="img"
      aria-label={t.agentBadge.label(name)}
      data-agent-badge="profile"
      data-icon={icon}
      data-color={color}
      className={cn(CHIP, PROFILE_COLOR_CLASSES[color], className)}
    >
      <ProfileGlyph icon={icon} />
      <span aria-hidden="true" className="truncate">
        {name}
      </span>
    </span>
  );

/**
 * Badge of the agent that did, or will do, a piece of work. With a known `profileId` it shows the
 * profile's appearance; otherwise it is neutral and shows `kind` (a Run's agent) or, without a kind,
 * that there is no profile.
 */
export function AgentBadge({
  profile
… 1483 characters omitted …
f a Run: the profile's badge followed by the provider and model it ran on
 * («claude · opus»); without a known profile, the neutral badge already carries that text.
 */
export function RunAgent({
  run,
}: {
  run: { profileId: string | null; agentKind: AgentKind; model?: string | null };
}) {
  const { find, loading } = useAgentProfiles();
  const text = formatAgent(run);
  if (loading && run.profileId) return <AgentBadge profileId={run.profileId} />;
  if (!find(run.profileId)) {
    return <AgentBadge profileId={null} kind={run.agentKind} label={text} />;
  }
  return (
    <span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <AgentBadge profileId={run.profileId} kind={run.agentKind} />
      <span>{text}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

  endRun(runId: string, patch: Partial<Json<RunView>>): void {

    Object.assign(run, { endedAt: this.now(), result: "success", ...patch });

+/**
+ * Resolves profiles by id from the shared `agent-profiles` query. `find` answers null for an id that
+ * no longer exists (and once the query failed), so callers fall back to the neutral badge. While the
+ * list loads, `loading` is true and callers show a placeholder instead of a wrong neutral badge.
+ */
+export function useAgentProfiles() {
+  const profiles = useQuery(queries.agentProfiles());
+  const byId = new Map((profiles.data ?? []).map((profile) => [profile.id, profile]));
+  return {
+    loading: profiles.isPending,
+    find: (profileId: string | null | undefined): Json<AgentProfile> | null =>
+      (profileId ? byId.get(profileId) : undefined) ?? null,
+  };
+}

// The mark of an agent profile (docs/decisions/agent-profiles.md §Appearance): its icon in a chip of
// its color followed by its name. A Run without profile, or whose profile was deleted, shows a neutral
// chip with its agent kind; work that has no profile at all shows a "no profile" mark.
import type { AgentKind, AgentProfile, ProfileColor, ProfileIcon } from "@lunasq/schemas";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import type { Json } from "../lib/api.ts";

import { queries } from "../lib/queries.ts";
import { cn } from "../lib/utils.ts";
import { NeutralGlyph, ProfileGl
… 4449 characters omitted …
 { profileId: string | null; agentKind: AgentKind; model?: string | null };
}) {
  const { find, loading, failed } = useAgentProfiles();
  const text = formatAgent(run);
  if (loading && run.profileId) return <AgentBadge profileId={run.profileId} />;
  if (failed && run.profileId) {
    return (
      <span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <AgentBadge profileId={run.profileId} />
        <span>{text}</span>
      </span>
    );
  }
  if (!find(run.profileId)) {
    return <AgentBadge profileId={null} kind={run.agentKind} label={text} />;
  }
  return (
    <span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <AgentBadge profileId={run.profileId} kind={run.agentKind} />
      <span>{text}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

**Agent** · 18:25:08

Verdict: **pass** for task f5bb4e72 (run 718efc80). LunasQ accepted it, so the phase is closed.

- **Round-2 must-fixes are fixed.** The «no profile» mark no longer shows while or after the profiles or Runs query fails (AC-9, AC-11, AC-14). Reverting that fix made 3 tests fail on their assertions.
- **All 15 criteria pass.** The suite is green (2980 passed, 41 skipped), and typecheck and lint are clean.
- **One gate failure, then green.** The first `submit_verdict` hit a red gate: 10 timeouts in unrelated web tests under load. Two local reruns were green, and the retry passed.
- **Minor findings, not blocking:**
  - The unavailable badge is a bare «—», so a failed query looks like "no agent needed".
  - An Inbox Run missing from a stale list still shows the no-profile mark.
  - Long profile names widen the tables instead of truncating.
  - The chip duplicates the badge variant classes.
  - The request-count test does not guard the `retryOnMount` fix.
  - Event log shows no badge while Runs load or fail.
- **Spec deviation.** The migration is `0022`, not `0021` as T-3 names it, because main took `0021`. The implementer did not list this under `specDeviations`.

The worktree still shows `lunasq/tasks/…md` as modified, as it was at the start. I did not touch it.

Goal: "Give agent profiles an icon and a color shown wherever agents work". Approved criteria:
AC-1 migration gives every existing profile an icon from PROFILE_ICONS and a color from PROFILE_COLORS, name and models unchanged.
AC-2 create without icon/color stores random ones from the sets and publishes agent_profile.created.
AC-3 icon/color outside the sets -> 400 invalid_input with path icon/color, nothing stored.
AC-4 update with only color changes it, keeps icon/name/models, publishes agent_profile.updated.
AC-5 update without icon/color keeps stored values.
AC-6 new profile dialog preselects icon and color, preview updates, sent on save.
AC-7 edit dialog sends new icon in PATCH; Profiles tab shows new badge.
AC-8 Runs table: each row shows its own Run's profile badge.
AC-9 Run with null or deleted profile shows neutral badge with agent kind; nothing fails.
AC-10 In progress Agent column shows latest Run's profile badge.
AC-11 Queue Agent column: badge of profile the project assigns to the next phase, or a no-profile mark.
AC-12 Event log: agent events show the attributed profile badge; human/system show none.
AC-13 Spec panel: agent-authored versions show the badge; human versions show none.
AC-14 Inbox: blocked agent shows its Run profile badge.
AC-15 labels from the active dictionary (es/en), accessible name with profile name, theme tokens for light and dark.

Known history: in round 2 the must-fixes were that the badge showed a wrong «no profile» mark while profiles or Runs loaded or failed (AC-9, AC-11, AC-14). }: ComponentProps<"span"> & VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants>) {
